Draft label review

All labels are AI-authored drafts. Review source support, completeness, scope and leakage. No candidate model identity or outputs are shown. Record independent reviews in separate sheets.

backup-01-Q01

How often does BACKUP-01 create a snapshot?

Split: development; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

backup-01-v2

# BACKUP-01: Recovery handbook / revision 2

Snapshots are created every 5 hours.
The recovery point objective is 3 minutes.
Snapshot copies expire after 30 days.
Restore rehearsals run in an isolated network with outbound access denied.
A restore is released only after the deletion ledger has been replayed.
The recovery operator records the snapshot checksum before starting the rehearsal.

backup-01-exception

# BACKUP-01: exception policy

When an incident is active, the incident commander must approve a restore rehearsal.

incident-01-Q01

What is the acknowledgement deadline for a severity-one INCIDENT-01 alert?

Split: development; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

incident-01-v2

# INCIDENT-01: Incident response policy / revision 2

A severity-one alert must be acknowledged within 5 minutes.
If the primary responder is unavailable, escalate to the secondary after 3 minutes.
Incident timelines are retained for 30 days.
The incident commander owns customer-facing status updates.
Declare recovery only after the error-rate alarm is clear for ten consecutive minutes.
The post-incident review records contributing conditions without assigning personal blame.

incident-01-exception

# INCIDENT-01: exception policy

For incidents involving customer records, the privacy liaison reviews the public update before publication.

incident-03-Q04

Who owns customer status updates during a INCIDENT-03 incident?

Split: development; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

incident-03-v2

# INCIDENT-03: Incident response policy / revision 2

A severity-one alert must be acknowledged within 9 minutes.
If the primary responder is unavailable, escalate to the secondary after 5 minutes.
Incident timelines are retained for 50 days.
The incident commander owns customer-facing status updates.
Declare recovery only after the error-rate alarm is clear for ten consecutive minutes.
The post-incident review records contributing conditions without assigning personal blame.

incident-03-exception

# INCIDENT-03: exception policy

For incidents involving customer records, the privacy liaison reviews the public update before publication.

Who was the named commander of yesterday's INCIDENT-05 incident?

Split: development; category: unanswerable; selected for independent second review: True

incident-05-v2

# INCIDENT-05: Incident response policy / revision 2

A severity-one alert must be acknowledged within 13 minutes.
If the primary responder is unavailable, escalate to the secondary after 7 minutes.
Incident timelines are retained for 70 days.
The incident commander owns customer-facing status updates.
Declare recovery only after the error-rate alarm is clear for ten consecutive minutes.
The post-incident review records contributing conditions without assigning personal blame.

incident-05-exception

# INCIDENT-05: exception policy

For incidents involving customer records, the privacy liaison reviews the public update before publication.

release-01-Q01

How long must the RELEASE-01 canary stay healthy before rollout expansion?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

release-01-v2

# RELEASE-01: Release control specification / revision 2

A canary must remain healthy for 5 minutes before expansion.
Stop a rollout when the five-minute error rate exceeds 3 percent.
The last signed release artifact remains available for 30 days.
A database migration must be backward compatible with the previous application version.
Rollback selects the last signed artifact; rebuilding the old source is not an accepted substitute.
A release candidate cannot bypass a failing tenant-isolation check.

release-01-exception

# RELEASE-01: exception policy

A release during a freeze requires approval from both the release owner and the incident commander.

The last signed release artifact remains available for 70 days.
A database migration must be backward compatible with the previous application version.
Rollback selects the last signed artifact; rebuilding the old source is not an accepted substitute.
A release candidate cannot bypass a failing tenant-isolation check.

release-05-exception

# RELEASE-05: exception policy

A release during a freeze requires approval from both the release owner and the incident commander.

billing-01-Q01

What is the maximum provider-cost reservation for one BILLING-01 request?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

billing-01-v2

# BILLING-01: Usage accounting contract / revision 2

A single request may reserve at most 5 cents of provider cost.
The workspace's daily provider budget is 3 dollars.
Usage ledger records are retained for 30 days.
Daily budget windows begin at midnight UTC.
If execution may have occurred but usage is unknown, charge the full reserved estimate.
Cached input tokens are a subset of total input tokens and are not added twice.

billing-01-exception

# BILLING-01: exception policy

A budget increase requires approval by a workspace owner; editors may view their own usage only.

# BILLING-02: Usage accounting contract / revision 2

A single request may reserve at most 7 cents of provider cost.
The workspace's daily provider budget is 4 dollars.
Usage ledger records are retained for 40 days.
Daily budget windows begin at midnight UTC.

A single request may reserve at most 11 cents of provider cost.

webhook-01-Q01

How many total delivery attempts may WEBHOOK-01 make for one webhook?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

webhook-01-v2

# WEBHOOK-01: Webhook delivery protocol / revision 2

Delivery is attempted at most 5 times including the original attempt.
The receiver must acknowledge a valid event within 3 seconds.
Delivered event identifiers remain in the deduplication ledger for 30 days.
Verify the signature over the original request bytes before decoding the JSON body.
HTTP 410 permanently disables that subscription; it is not retried.
An event identifier is stable across all retry deliveries.

webhook-01-exception

# WEBHOOK-01: exception policy

A subscription disabled by HTTP 410 can be reenabled only by its workspace owner after endpoint verification.

keys-01-Q01

What is the signing-credential rotation interval for KEYS-01?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

keys-01-v2

# KEYS-01: Credential rotation procedure / revision 2

Signing credentials rotate every 5 days.
The old verification key remains accepted for a 3-minute overlap.
Rotation audit metadata is retained for 30 days.
The private signing key is mounted from a secret file and never placed in browser configuration.
After confirmed compromise, revoke the old key immediately and skip the normal overlap.
Audit events contain the key identifier and action, but never private key bytes.

keys-01-exception

# KEYS-01: exception policy

An emergency rotation requires two distinct security operators to approve the new key identifier.
Proposed labels — unreviewed

The old verification key remains accepted for a 3-minute overlap.

The private signing key is mounted from a secret file and never placed in browser configuration.
After confirmed compromise, revoke the old key immediately and skip the normal overlap.

retention-01-Q01

When do completed RETENTION-01 conversation messages expire?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: True

retention-01-v2

# RETENTION-01: Document lifecycle policy / revision 2

Completed conversation messages expire after 5 days.
Private originals are physically purged within 3 hours after a deletion request.
Encrypted recovery backups are retained for 30 days.
A deletion tombstone blocks new source reads immediately, before physical erasure completes.
Saved answers referencing a deleted source are hidden on the next authorized read.
A restore replays independent deletion records before accepting application traffic.

retention-01-exception

# RETENTION-01: exception policy

A legal hold delays physical erasure of the selected original but never restores ordinary application access.
Proposed labels — unreviewed

Private originals are physically purged within 5 hours after a deletion request.
Encrypted recovery backups are retained for 50 days.
A deletion tombstone blocks new source reads immediately, before physical erasure completes.
Saved answers referencing a deleted source are hidden on the next authorized read.
A restore replays independent deletion records before accepting application traffic.

rate_limit-01-Q01

What per-minute mutation limit applies to a RATE_LIMIT-01 actor?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: True

rate_limit-01-v2

# RATE_LIMIT-01: Request admission protocol / revision 2

An authenticated actor may submit at most 5 mutation requests per minute.
At most 3 generation jobs may run concurrently in one workspace.
Idempotency records remain valid for 30 hours.
A reused idempotency key with a changed body returns a conflict instead of starting new work.
Capacity rejection occurs before provider transport or cost reservation.
Retry-After expresses the earliest safe retry delay in whole seconds.

rate_limit-01-exception

# RATE_LIMIT-01: exception policy

An operator capacity exception may raise a workspace concurrency limit but cannot bypass tenant authorization.

rate_limit-05-Q06

What unit and meaning does RATE_LIMIT-05's Retry-After value have?

Split: test; category: direct; selected for independent second review: False

rate_limit-05-v2

# RATE_LIMIT-05: Request admission protocol / revision 2

An authenticated actor may submit at most 13 mutation requests per minute.
At most 7 generation jobs may run concurrently in one workspace.
Idempotency records remain valid for 70 hours.
A reused idempotency key with a changed body returns a conflict instead of starting new work.
Capacity rejection occurs before provider transport or cost reservation.
Retry-After expresses the earliest safe retry delay in whole seconds.

rate_limit-05-exception

# RATE_LIMIT-05: exception policy

An operator capacity exception may raise a workspace concurrency limit but cannot bypass tenant authorization.
